Actual Answer
Soft-Touch Medical Grade Electrodes (Soft-Touch Cloth Electrodes, PMT gel) are self-adhesive, reusable, high-quality gel electrodes designed for use with TENS and EMS devices.

Key features and specifications PMT provides:

• Product name: Soft-Touch Medical Grade Electrodes – Soft-Touch Cloth Electrodes (PMT gel)
• Product code: FA2020 (2.0" x 2.0" size)
• Electrode type: Self-adhesive, reusable disposable electrodes with a pig tail pin connector
• Compatibility: Universal for almost all TENS and EMS devices, as well as IF and Russian units, and back treatments, that use the pin-type connector
• Resistance: 45 ohms of resistance
• Reusability: 10–15 or more uses per pad on average (depending on length of use)
• Backing options: Available with a carbon and a blue cloth backing
• Corner design: Oval corners to help minimize edge curl
• Performance: Delivers great dispersion and durability; value priced
• Gel: High quality PMT gel; for best results, replace electrodes after the conductive gel starts to wear down (typically after 10–15 uses per pad)
• Packaging: Resealable pouch to help extend electrode life
• Sizes and pack options:
  – 2.0" x 2.0" – qty: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
  – 2.0" Round – qty: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
  – 3" Round – 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
  – Butterfly 6" x 3.3" – qty: 10 packs of 1 electrode per pack
• Product weight: 1 lb per listed configuration
• Size (example spec): 2.0" x 2.0" (width approx. 5 cm)
• Weight code: G

Care and lifespan information:
• Electrode life-span and effectiveness can be enhanced by rubbing a small drop of water on the pad after use.
• Always keep electrodes in their air-tight (resealable) package directly after use.
• For best therapy results, PMT recommends replacing electrodes once the conductive gel starts to wear down (usually after about 10–15 uses per pad).

Actual Answer
Rapid Knee (Rigid Wrap-on Knee brace) – Small from PMT is part of the Rapid Knee RK200 series (Rigid Wrap-on Knee brace). While the Knowledge Base specifically lists Medium and XXXL, the features, design, and use are the same across all sizes, including Small.

Key points you should know:

• Product name/series: Rapid Knee (Rigid Wrap-on Knee brace)
• Product code family: RK200 (Small size is available in Right or Left)
• Fit options by size: Small – Right or Left (also available in Medium, Large, XL, 2XL, 3XL – Right or Left)

Main features:
• COMFORTABLE – Comfort‑tech material allows for a very comfortable fit.
• ADJUSTABLE – The side hinge opens and allows for the extension and flexion to be adjusted, limiting the range of motion of the knee as needed.
• EASY ACCESS – Rear access, open in the front, allows the brace to be wrapped on instead of slipped on if desired (helpful if you have limited mobility or post‑surgical bandaging).
• LIGHTWEIGHT – Super light and easy to wear.
• GUARANTEED – Satisfaction guaranteed.

Description:
• Double‑upright hinges with wrap‑on access and adjustable flexion/extension hinges.
• Easy‑access wrap‑on straps for secure, even support.

Specifications for the RK200 series (applies across sizes):
• Product Weight (lbs): 2
• Width (cms): 7
• HCPCS Code: L1845 / L1852
• Weight Code: G

Use & operation (applies to Rapid Knee wrap-on style braces):
• The Rapid Knee brace is sized based on knee circumference – you select Small if your knee measurement falls in the Small range provided by your clinician or supplier.
• While in a sitting position, the brace can be unwrapped (outer straps and inner straps opened) and slipped or wrapped onto the leg and knee.
• The patella (kneecap) cut‑out area should be centered over the front of the knee.
• Front ends/straps are then Velcro‑closed to create comfortable compression.
• The Range of Motion (ROM) hinge can be:
  – Left open for a more complete range of motion, or
  – Adjusted to set specific degrees of flexion and/or extension, or
  – Stopped/locked at a certain point to help manage contractures or limit motion per your clinician’s guidance.
• The adjustable hinge is protected by a cover so it does not interfere with the other knee and helps prevent an adjustment pin from slipping out of position.

Indications for use (conditions for which these Rapid Knee braces are typically prescribed):
1) Recent injury to or surgical procedure on the knee
2) Rheumatoid arthritis
3) Osteoarthritis
4) Meniscal cartilage derangement
5) Chondromalacia of the patella
6) Knee ligamentous disruption
7) Rupture of tendon, non‑traumatic – quadriceps tendon
8) Pathologic fracture of femur
9) Pathologic fracture of tibia or fibula
10) Aseptic necrosis of tibia or fibula
11) Stress fracture of tibia or fibula
12) Congenital deformity of knee
13) Fracture of femur – lower end
14) Fracture (other listed knee‑related fractures per your prescribing clinician)

If you’re unsure whether Small is the correct size for you or how to set the hinge for your specific condition, please follow the sizing and adjustment instructions from your prescribing healthcare professional or contact PMT directly for product‑specific sizing guidance.

Actual Answer
The Wobble Stool from Pain Management Technologies is a self-balancing ergonomic stool designed to promote healthy posture and help reduce back and neck discomfort associated with long periods of sitting.

Key details from PMT:

• Product Name: The Wobble Stool – Self Balancing Stool with 360 Degree Rotation  
• Product Code: WS150  
• Color: Black  
• Product Weight: 1 lb (lightweight and portable)

Description & Features:

• Balancing Wobble Stool – Designed for people who suffer from back pain that is often the result of poor posture, especially after long days at the office or sitting for extended periods. The Wobble Stool helps you sit up straight no matter how you position yourself on it.

• Promotes Positive Posture – The stool is designed to naturally steer your body into an upright position just by sitting down, providing an effortless way to improve posture. As the chair tilts in any direction, you can feel your center of gravity and your body will automatically correct your position as a natural response.

• Full 360 Degree Tilt & Rotation – The tilt technology allows the Wobble Stool to rotate and tilt in any direction, following the pressure applied by your movement and positioning. This unique design lets the stool adapt to each individual’s posture and balance and can help reduce pain associated with poor posture.

• Lightweight and Portable – The Wobble Stool is a perfect addition to any chair at work or at home. It is designed to be lightweight and portable so you can take it with you on the go. Users typically notice a difference when sitting to work for long periods as posture improves.

• Durable Comfort – Constructed with high-quality materials to stand up to the consistent impact and pressure from hours of sitting and standing. It includes a thick foam cushion for optimal comfort during use. The overall experience is meant to be simple, easy, and comfortable.

If you’d like, I can also share details on the related Wobble Stool Roller Chair (Product Code: WS250), which adds a rolling base with similar self-balancing, 360° tilt-and-rotate functionality.

Actual Answer
Travel UltraLite Powered Wheelchair by MobiJoe, 500W Dual Motor - Lightweight Mobility for Seniors, Foldable and Compact for Easy Travel and Storage. Includes 2 Li-ion Batteries.

LIGHTWEIGHT AND COMPACT DESIGN: The MobiJoe is designed to be lightweight and meant to come with you wherever you need it. The ultra compact, folding design means that you can take the MobiJoe wherever you need it. When folded, the chair can be conveniently stored into any trunk space with ease. Stay mobile with the MobiJoe!

TRAVEL WITH POWER: Our wheelchair features a powerful motor system with shock absorption springs and the ability to travel over 10 miles on a single charge. The quick charging battery returns to full power within 6-8 hours so you can keep traveling with freedom.

BUILT TO LAST: The MobiJoe Travel UltraLite combines portability with durability. It consists of a tough aluminum alloy frame that is still lightweight and can support over 260 pounds. Our electric wheelchairs utilize an electromagnetic braking system to ensure a long lasting construction.

EASY CONTROL: Our joystick style controller allows for easy operation with simple controls. The joystick directional controls enable the chair to turn immediately at the user’s discretion in order to safely navigate everyday obstacles. Don’t let any obstacles get in your way of living to the fullest!

STAY PREPARED: The wheelchair comes packed with a charger, 24V lithium ion battery, a seat cushion and a safety belt. In addition, it can also be equipped with travel bags or extra storage if necessary, with enough carrying capacity to support the extra supplies. Bring everything you need along with you so you can keep moving!

PRODUCT CODE: MJUL

WARRANTY

DESCRIPTION
The Travel UltraLite by MobiJoe is a premium powered wheelchair designed to be compact and powerful. Firm construction and lightweight frame allow the wheelchair to be folded up and packed away with ease. The Electromagnetic braking system, combined with a joystick controller that can be placed on either side of the chair, allows the user to feel completely in control of the MobiJoe. Travel up to 13 miles on a single battery charge and return to full power within 6-8 hours so you can keep moving the whole day.

Technical Specifications:
Material - Aluminum alloy
Folding Size - 72in * 80in * 40in
Battery Specs - 10.4Ah * li-ion battery
Front Wheels - 8” * 2”
Rear Wheels - 10” * 2”
Weight - 40.3lbs
SPECIFICATIONS
Product Weight (lbs) : 1

Actual Answer
Electrotherapy probe PR-06A is a Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe designed for use with compatible pelvic muscle stimulators.

Here is the PMT information about this probe:

• Product name: Electrotherapy probe PR-06A – Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe  
• Intended use: Specially designed for treating urinary incontinence and urinary problems. It is used as part of pelvic floor (pelvis muscle) stimulation therapy.

• Design & fit:
  - Completely fits with the body curve  
  - Completely adheres/pastes on the treatment region  
  - Eases muscle for better contraction

• Compatibility:
  - Effectively equipped with various incontinence and pelvis muscle stimulators  
  - Designed to be used with incontinence stimulators, womancare devices, or other pelvis muscle stimulators whose output is less than 0.25 W/cm²  
  - Please note: These probes must be used in tandem with an EMS device in order to attain desired results (they are not stand‑alone devices).

• Safety characteristics:
  - High safety and steadiness  
  - No over‑stimulation on the treatment region (within specified device output limits)

• Product code and model:
  - Product Code: PR06A  
  - Model: Vaginal Small

• Indications:
  - Urinary incontinence  
  - Urinary problems

• Basic specification:
  - Product Weight (lbs): 1

This probe is an accessory used with a suitable EMS/pelvic floor stimulator; it does not provide therapy on its own. For specific setup, usage instructions, or whether it is appropriate for your situation, please refer to your device manual or consult a qualified healthcare professional.

Actual Answer
Here’s a concise summary of PMT’s information related to its cold water therapy pads and couplings for cryotherapy systems (these are water therapy pads, not TENS skin electrodes):

1. **Cold Water Therapy Coupling Converter (Product Code: TP2020-CC)**
- Purpose: Converts many existing cryotherapy / water therapy units so they can use **PMT brand water therapy pads**.
- Use: Replace existing fittings on your current unit with PMT’s quick-connect/disconnect couplings.
- Benefits:
  - Helps fix leak-prone competitor connections by providing a more secure, less leaky attachment.
  - Allows compatibility with PMT’s specialized pads across many circulating water therapy systems (hot or cold).
- Therapy Role:
  - Used with hot or cold water therapy units.
  - Supports cooling relief to help reduce swelling and manage discomfort from sports injuries, surgery recovery, and some chronic issues.
  - Circulating cold water allows localized, stable therapy compared with loose ice packs.

2. **Cold Water Therapy Couplings for Arctic Ice Clear (Product Code: TP2020-CC-White)**
- Purpose: Replacement coupling set for **Arctic Ice Clear, Arctic Medical, Coolman, and other water therapy units**.
- Function:
  - Works as a converter so these systems can use **PMT water therapy pads**.
  - Uses quick-connect/disconnect couplings to improve connection security and reduce leaks.
- Application:
  - For cold therapy units that circulate water to help reduce swelling and provide cooling relief for post-op, sports, and some chronic conditions.

3. **Cold Water Therapy Pad – Universal Pad (Product Code: TPU-New)**
- Compatibility:
  - **Compatible** with Arctic Ice Clear directly.
  - **Not compatible** with Arctic Ice Classic, ARS, Polar Vortex, Polar Sport **without a coupling converter**.
  - A **coupling converter may be required** for units from other manufacturers.
- Design & Features:
  - Designed for hot or cold water therapy units.
  - Provides **continuous water flow** that circumferentially covers and treats the target body area.
  - Includes **leak-proof quick connect/disconnect couplings** to easily change pads and maintain a leak-free connection.
  - Includes an **insulation hose** for increased R-value (helps maintain water temperature) and a **strap** to secure the pad over the treatment area.
- Use Case:
  - Facilitates optimal water circulation for efficient treatment of acute and chronic disabilities.

4. **Cold Water Therapy Pad – Face Pad (Product Code: FC)**
- Compatibility:
  - **Compatible** with Arctic Ice Classic, ARS, Polar Vortex, and Polar Sport.
  - **Coupling converter required** for Arctic Ice Clear and many other manufacturers’ units.
- Design & Features:
  - Face-shaped pad designed for use with hot or cold water therapy units.
  - Provides **continuous flow** across the facial area to circumferentially treat the affected region.
  - **Leak-proof quick connect/disconnect couplings** for easy pad changes and secure, drip-resistant connections.
  - Includes an **insulation hose** for better thermal efficiency and a **strap** for securing to the area of need.
- Use Case:
  - Supports efficient water flow for treating acute and chronic facial-related conditions with targeted cold (or hot) therapy.

If you were specifically looking for **TENS skin electrodes** (stick-on pads for an electrical TENS unit) instead of water therapy pads for cryotherapy machines, please tell me the exact device name (for example, “Ultima 5 TENS” or “Ultima 20”) and I can summarize PMT’s information for the correct electrodes.

Actual Answer
Pain Management Technologies (PMT) does not list a "Jaw Traction TENS unit" as a single combined device in the available product information. However, PMT does offer:

1) Jaw traction / jaw exerciser device – Jawfit (Product Code: JF100)
- Category: Traction / jaw exerciser, not a TENS unit.
- Main purposes:
  • Double chin reducer: helps minimize the appearance of a double chin, tighten facial muscles, and define the outline of the face.
  • Helps with mouth breathers: can help minimize mouth-only breathing by strengthening masticatory (chewing) muscles and may help improve dry mouth.
  • Mouth and facial issues: listed benefits include reduction of halitosis (bad breath), stomatitis, acne, and pimples, realigning facial symmetry, and minimizing eyestrain.
  • Chewing/swallowing assistance: aims to help correct difficulty swallowing and/or drooling/slobbering; may also be used to help reduce cravings.
- Materials and safety:
  • BPA free, food-grade safe plastic.
  • Designed in Japan.
- How it works (exercise concept):
  • The Jawfit is an exerciser, not an electrical or TENS device.
  • It activates several facial and neck-related muscles, including masticatory muscles, tongue muscles, orbicularis oculi, soft palate and fauces, suprahyoid, infrahyoid, strap muscles, cervical area, and pharyngeal muscles.
  • It is intended as a “workout” for the jaw and related muscles: you need to make a repeated “kissing” motion while using it to create muscle exercise.
  • Over time, it is designed to exercise your jaw, help refine a double chin, strengthen facial muscles, and support firmer skin.

2) TENS units (general information from PMT)
- A Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation (TENS) unit is a small, battery-powered device that sends low-voltage electrical pulses through electrode pads placed on the skin.
- These gentle electrical impulses stimulate nerves under the skin and can:
  • Help block pain signals before they reach the brain.
  • Encourage release of endorphins (your body’s natural pain relievers).
- Intended use:
  • TENS is used to help manage pain; it is not designed to cure underlying conditions or heal tissue directly.
  • It can provide temporary relief from discomfort associated with issues such as back pain, arthritis, sciatica, muscle soreness, and joint pain.

Important clarification:
- The Jawfit jaw traction/exerciser (JF100) itself is **not** a TENS unit and does not deliver electrical stimulation.
- PMT TENS units are separate electrotherapy devices used for nerve stimulation and pain management on various body areas (e.g., back, neck, shoulders, joints) using electrode pads.

If you were asking specifically how to use a TENS unit **on or around the jaw**, that type of pad placement and safety guidance is not included in the provided PMT information. For any jaw- or face-area use with a TENS unit, you should follow the device’s official manual and consult a licensed healthcare professional before applying electrodes near the head, face, or neck area.

Actual Answer
The Electric Back with 1 – 4x10 Dual Electrode from Pain Management Technologies (PMT) is a high-end electrotherapy-compatible back compression brace designed to work with fabric conductive electrodes.

Key points:

• **Product name:** Electric Back with 1 – 4x10 Dual Electrode  
• **Product code:** EB1-410  
• **Category:** Electrotherapy Garments / Back brace with conductive electrode

**How it works:**  
- The brace uses tension pulls and is designed to be used in conjunction with fabric conductive electrodes.  
- The fabric electrodes Velcro into the brace and are then compressed against the treatment site so therapy can be delivered through your connected electrotherapy device (such as a TENS/EMS unit that uses compatible conductive patches).  
- The Electric Back accommodates conductive patches via hook-and-loop (Velcro) so you can position the patches where you need them and adjust as needed.

**Use with and without electrotherapy:**  
- When used with electrotherapy: you attach the conductive patches inside the brace, position them over the target area, and connect them to your stimulation unit as directed for your device. The brace’s compression helps keep the electrodes in place.  
- Without electrotherapy: you can remove the conductive patches and use the Electric Back purely as a fully functioning active back brace for compression and support.

**Capacity / channels:**  
- The Electric Back can accommodate **2–4 conductive patches**.  
- This allows **both TENS channels** from a dual‑channel unit to be used in a single brace by utilizing two double-strip patches (4x10 style).

**Brace quality and design:**  
- High-end compression workman’s-style back brace.  
- Designed for active use while providing compression and electrode positioning.

**Size and dimensions (EB1-410 version):**  
- Available sizes: **MD–LG** and **LG–XL**  
- Length: **42 inches relaxed**, stretches to **47 inches**  
- Width: **6 cm**  
- Product weight: **1 lb**  
- Weight code: **G**

If you’d like, I can also help you compare this EB1-410 version to the Electric Back with 2 – 4x10 Dual Electrodes (EB2-410), which is longer and accommodates a different size range.

The brace’s compression helps keep the electrodes in place.  \n- Without electrotherapy: you can remove the conductive patches and use the Electric Back purely as a fully functioning active back brace for compression and support.\n\n**Capacity / channels:**  \n- The Electric Back can accommodate **2–4 conductive patches**.  \n- This allows **both TENS channels** from a dual‑channel unit to be used in a single brace by utilizing two double-strip patches (4x10 style).\n\n**Brace quality and design:**  \n- High-end compression workman’s-style back brace.  \n- Designed for active use while providing compression and electrode positioning.\n\n**Size and dimensions (EB1-410 version):**  \n- Available sizes: **MD–LG** and **LG–XL**  \n- Length: **42 inches relaxed**, stretches to **47 inches**  \n- Width: **6 cm**  \n- Product weight: **1 lb**  \n- Weight code: **G**\n\nIf you’d like, I can also help you compare this EB1-410 version to the Electric Back with 2 – 4x10 Dual Electrodes (EB2-410), which is longer and accommodates a different size range."}
